NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
TUESDAY, February 12, 2013 
 
PRESENT: Sylvia Allen, Supervisor; Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor; David Tenney, Supervisor; Jesse 
Thompson, Supervisor; and Jonathan M. Nez, Chairman were present in person.  
9:05 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation were led by Supervisor Whitesinger.  
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Terry Hill, the Legislative Chairman for VFW Post 9907 in Show Low and his wife 
Joyce Hill, the Legislative Chairman for the Ladies Auxiliary  VFW Post 9907 addressed the Board regarding 
Congresswoman Ann Kirkpatrick’s visit to VFW Post 9907 on Monday at 1:00 p.m. to talk wi th veterans in the 
community. He addressed HB2466 which is before the Arizona House of Representatives that was submitted by 
Bob Thorpe, District 6 Representative. This bill would amend the statute to require law enforcement and judges 
to receive training on PTSD.  He requested support on helping to get the Bill HB2466 passed by speak ing to 
representatives and help with any funding that may be available . The County Attorney spoke about  the 
Veteran’s Board last meeting and the VFW is very supportive . He indicated that on Saturday there will be a 
public hearing in Snowflake on Anti Agenda 21 and the 2nd Amendment. 
 
CONSENT AGENDA : a)Minutes: January 22, 2013  b) Clerk of Superior Court Report January 2013  c) 
Juvenile Probation Report January 2013  d) Justice Court Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, January; Winslow 
Precinct #2, January; Snowflake-Taylor Precinct #3, January; Kayenta Precinct #4, January; Show Low Precinct 
#5, January; and Pinetop -Lakeside Precinct #6, January 2013 e) Constable Reports: Show Low Precinct #5, 
January 2013 f) Assessment and Tax Roll Corrections for data tax year 2012  g) Resolution Number 06 -13, 
authorizing the Arizona Attorney General’s office to provide legal representation to Navajo County in several 
tax appeals involving centrally assessed property, specifically, Transwestern Pipeline v. Navajo County ADOR, 
et al., TX -2010-000067/TX-2013-000241, Cable One v. Navajo County, ADOR, et. al., TX -2012-000805, 
Snowflake Power v. Navajo Coun ty, ADOR, et. al., TX2012 -000097, TX -2012-000567 h)Award contract to 
Tate’s Auto Direct for purchase of 2013 Ford Escape in the amount of $27,825.54  i) Re-Appointment of the 
David Antonini, LuAnn Garbini, Dana Sipes, Yvonne Hunt, Paul Julien, Jeanne Boyce,  Shirley Patterson, Anna 
Lesperance, Lisa Maestas, Jay Rominger and Ron Oberholtzer as Justices of the Peace Pro Tempore for the 
Navajo County Justice Courts j) Re-Appointment of Bob Hall as an alternate member to the Planning & Zoning 
Commission for District II k) Re-Appointment of Randy Murph as a regular member to the Planning & Zoning 
Commission for District III  l) Amendment #3 to Lone Pine Dam Road Special Use Permit #LAK101662  m) 
Name an existing road in the Pinedale/Claysprings area to “Jasmine Lane”  in Section 18, Township 11 North, 
Range 19 East  n) Name an existing road in the Pinedale/Claysprings area to “Azalea Lane” in Section 18, 
Township 11 North, Range 19 East o) Letter of support for Tribal Airports  p) Letter in support of the City of 
Show Low’s CDBG Grants q) Sympathy letter to the family of Becky Barris r) Warrant & Voucher Report over 
$1000 for January 2013: Supervisor Thompson made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the items in 
the consent agenda as  presented; motion seconded by  Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.  
 
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS: Consideration and possible approval of a Proclamation declaring February 12, 
2013 as “Day of the Cougars” (David Tenney)  Supervisor Tenney indicated that d ue to the weather he would 
ask that this item be moved to February 26th.

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 02-12-2013 
Page 2 of 5 
 
 EMPLOYEE RECOGNITION: Navajo County Recognizes Brent Turley, Lilene Sanchez, Cameron Crandell 
and Anna Ash  for their excellence in personal performance far exceeding organization expectations : Pam 
Phillips recognized Cameron Crandell, Anna Ash and Lilene Sanchez. Brent Turley will be recognized next 
month due to medical leave. Pam spoke of the personal performance of each employee. Mr. Jayne thanked 
each employee, the employee recognition team and the strategic planning team.   
HUMAN RESOURCES:  
a. Personnel Service Awards: Kimberly Eavenson recognized Eric Baldonado, Jill Hunt, William Johnson, 
Cory Knight, Scot Martin Jr, Eugenia Gree son, and Kelly King for their 5 years of service to Navajo 
County; Lorin Larson, Norma Willard, Judge  Stephen Price, and Phyllis Romo  for their  10 years  of 
service; Allen Payne and Martinez Mike  for their 15 years of service; Supervisor Jesse Thompson for 
his 20 years of service to Navajo County.  
b. Consideration and pos sible approval of Personnel Actions : Kimberly Eavenson reviewed the 24 item 
personnel action report. Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to approve the personnel action report as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
SILVER CREEK FLOOD PROTECTION DISTRICT : Consideration and possible appointment of Will 
Flake to fill the vacancy on the Silver Creek Flood Protection District Board : Ron Solomon discussed the item  
indicating that the vacancy was made by the passing of Mr. Jim Bruce and  would like to fill the vacancy with 
Mr. Will Flake. Supervisor Allen made a motion to appoint Will Flake to fill the vacancy on the Silver Creek 
Flood Protection District Board ; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous appr oving the 
motion. 
ASSESSOR: Presentation of Assessors Valuation and Notice of Value: Cammy Darris gave a brief presentation 
of the Assessors Valuation and Notice of Value and answered questions of the Board. Notice of Values will go 
out next Friday. Mr. Jayne made comments and invited everyone to go to the Assessor’s website.  
    BOARD OF SUPERVISORS acting as BOARD OF EQUALIZATION : Supervisor Tenney made a 
motion to enter into the Board of Equalization; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
a. BOARD OF EQUALIZATION : Consideration and possible approval of the property owners’ appeal 
to re -classify property to class three properties (owner occupied), waive assessed penalties an d liens 
related to this recent reclassification action, and direct the County Assessor to re -classify the properties 
to class three (owner occupied), pursuant to A.R.S. §41 -12003 for the following properties: APN 309 -
42-114 & APN 207 -16-051F8: Melissa Buck ley discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney made a 
motion to approve the property owners’ appeal to re -classify property to class three properties (owner 
occupied), waive assessed penalties and liens related to this recent reclassification action, and direct  the 
County Assessor to re -classify the properties to class three (owner occupied), pursuant to A.R.S. §41 -
12003 for the following properties: APN 309 -42-114 & APN 207 -16-051F8; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Allen. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. BOARD OF EQUALIZATION : Consideration and possible denial of the property owner's appeal to 
re-classify property which has not satisfied the requirements of occupancy status, and maintain property 
classification at class four properties (non -owner occupied). The class four property classification (non -
owner occupied), shall remain in effect Pursuant to A.R.S. §42 -12052 for the following property: APN 
309-42-113: Melissa Buckley discussed the item and asked that this item be approved to re -classify the 
property due to new information that the owner receives his mail at his neighbors house however that 
this is his primary residence . Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to approve the property owners’

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 02-12-2013 
Page 3 of 5 
 
appeal to re -classify property to class three properties (owner occup ied), waive assessed penalties and 
liens related to this recent reclassification action, and direct the County Assessor to re -classify the 
properties to class three (owner occupied), pursuant to A.R.S. §41 -12003 for the following property: 
APN 309-42-113; motion seconded by Supervisor Allen. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by  Supervisor Thompson. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
TREASURER: Consideration and possible approval of Personal Property Tax abatements per A.R.S. §42 -
19118: Manny Hernandez discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to approve Personal 
Property Tax abatements per A.R.S. §42 -19118; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
CONSTABLES: Consideration and possible approval of two grants from the Constable Ethics and Training 
Board: Constable Scott Tipton discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve two grants 
from the Constable Ethi cs and Training Board ; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
FINANCE:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of a purchase of (4) Dodge 1500 4x4 Crew Cab Pick -up Trucks 
including police package in the amount of $169,593.28 from Avondale Automotive, Inc. : James 
Menlove  discussed the item and requested approval. Supervisor Allen asked what the Sheriff does with 
the old vehicles. James Menlove indicated they are recycled or sold at auction with the  monies going 
back to the funding source. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the purchase of (4) Dodge 
1500 4x4 Crew Cab Pick -up Trucks including police package in the amount of $169,593.28 from 
Avondale Automotive, Inc. ; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving 
the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible award of a one year contract for collection servi ces to A/R Collection 
Services: James Menlove recognized Ms. Lena Alley who  put this contract together. Mr. Menlove 
discussed the i tem. Supervisor Allen inquired about the items that peo ple are not payin g. Mr. Menlove 
replied that these are unnecessary calls to the Sheriff’s office, deputies who do not fulfill their contracts 
or when something is due to the county that we cannot collect we would  use the collection service . 
Supervisor Allen made a motion to award a one year contract for collection services to A/R Collection 
Services; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
PUBLIC WORKS:  
a. Consideration and possib le award of Contract #B12 -12-012, purchase of (1) or more Water Tanker 
Trailers (6,000 – 6,500 Gallon) to Construction Trucks & Equipment and approval to purchase (2) 
Water Tanker Trailers: Homero Vela discussed the item and requested approval. Supervisor Allen asked 
if these were already budgeted for.  Homero Vela responded  yes they have been budgeted . Supervisor 
Thompson made a motion to award Contract #B12 -12-012, purchase of (1) or more  Water Tanker 
Trailers (6,000 – 6,500 Gallon) to Construction Trucks & Equipment and approval to purchase (2) 
Water Tanker Trailers; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible award of Contract #B 12-12-017, purchase of (1) or more Backhoe Loader to 
RDO Equipment Company for the purchase of a 2013 John Deere, model number 310SK : Homero Vela 
discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to award Contract #B12-12-017, purchase 
of (1) or mor e Backhoe Loader to RDO Equipment Company for the purchase of a 2013 John Deere, 
model number 310SK; motion seconded by Supervisor Allen. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 4 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 02-12-2013 
Page 4 of 5 
 
c. Consideration and possible approval to purchase steel and miscellaneous hardware  to build Cinder 
Spreader Hangers: Homero Vela discussed the item. Supervisor Allen made a motion to approve the  
purchase of steel and miscellaneous hardware to build Cinder Spreader Hangers ; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY ATTORNEY:  
a. EXECUTIVE SESSION: pursuant to A.R.S. §38 -431.03(A) (3) for the Board to receive legal advice 
from its attorneys regarding Settlement of Snowflake Power LLC. v. Navajo County Litigation/TX2010-
000990: Jason Moore requested the Board to enter into executive session. Supervisor Thompson made 
a motion to enter into executive session;  motion seconded by  Supervisor Allen . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion. At 10:15 a.m. the Board retired into executive session. Discussion was held.  
b. RECONVENE: for discussion and possible Board action regarding Settlement of Snowflake Power 
LLC. v. Navajo Count y Litigation/TX2010-000990: At 10:23 a.m. the Board reconvened. Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to return to regular session ; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to accept the Settlement-counter 
offer as recommended by the Attorney General’s Office regarding Settlement of Snowflake Power LLC. 
v. Navajo County Litigation/TX2010 -000990; Motion seconded by Supervisor Allen. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE : Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Allen thanked County Manager Jayne and the Navajo County employees and indicated that she is 
very happy to serve as part of the Navajo County team and to serve the people.  She spoke of further erosion of 
our economy in Navajo County and is disappointed in the closing of Snowfl ake Power. She is very concerned 
of how the Euro Fresh bankruptcy will affect the greenhouse in Snowflake . She is also concerned on how to  
help economic development in our county and sees RealAz and working with Apache County as a valuable tool 
to help our  economic growth. She indicated that see will attend a  meeting regarding the Mexican Wolf 
introduction and the expansion which takes in areas of Arizona and New Mexico  and indicated that she will 
attend a  Town Hall  meeting in Snowflake  in regards to A genda 21 and our Second Amendment rights . 
Supervisor Whitesinger is grateful for the County and their support of her new path of being a service provider 
and a community advocate. She is concerned with the Snowflake jobs and challenges that face our county and is 
grateful for the projects  she is working on to continue the  support of McNary Library and their community . 
Supervisor Tenney continues to lobby at the Capitol  and met with the Speaker of the House  regarding county 
issues, the counties assistance fund, and the restoring of  HURF funds. He also thanked the public w orks team 
for their snow removal  efforts. Supervisor Thompson spoke of NACOG TPAC and their resolution regarding 
returning HURF monies back to the counties and he attended  Indian Nations and Stat e Legislative Day. Mr. 
Jayne attended  the Hashknife Pony Express sendoff dinner and  accepted a picture presented to  the Board. 
Chairman Nez reported on District I regarding the Navajo Nation declared emergency issues due to freezing 
temperatures and t hanked the State of Arizona and public w orks for helping out in Low Mountain  and other 
areas.  He rep orted about his meetings in Washington visiting  with Congresswoman Kirkpatrick, Congressman 
Gosar, Congressman Jason Chaffetz from Utah and meetings  with all con gressional delegations that cover the 
Navajo Nation in Arizona, Utah and New Mexico regarding HURF funding, school bus funding, transportation 
and infrastructure issues and the Winslow Levee .  He mentioned the February 19 meeting in Window Rock of 
the Navajo Nation Law and Order C ommittee for  the Cross Commissio ning Law Enforcemen t Agreement 
between the Navajo N ation and the Navajo County Sheriff’s Office . Supervisor Thompson reported that the 
First Mesa Consolidated Village is very appreciative of the assistance given with their broken water line  pipes 
and laying down gravel on road sites.

<!-- page 5 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 02-12-2013 
Page 5 of 5 
 
Supervisor Tenney and the Board sang Happy Birthday to James Menlove for Friday, February 15.  
 
Navajo word of the day:  Nizhoni meaning beautiful.  
ADJOURN: at 10:45 a.m. Supervisor Allen  made a motion to adjourn ; motion seconded by  Supervisor 
Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
 
 
 
 
 
APPROVED: 
 
 Jonathan M. Nez, Chairman 
 
 
ATTEST:  
 
 
 
 
DATE:  February 26, 2013 
 
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board
